Revision history

RevisionDateWhat changed and why
A2026-10-25Initial static inventory and bounded cold source observations to prioritize missing report evidence.

Rule basis

  • L1: UTF-8 text reads, LF writes and a test run with PYTHONUTF8 unset
  • L2: External equation, coefficient and factor citations with edition and clause
  • L3: Three reported numbers with literal, first-principles hand workings
  • L4: Numerical tolerances or displayed rounding; actionable rebuild failures
  • L5: Claims bounded by retained revision and collected-test evidence
  • L6: Draft for maintainer review and public wording
  • L7: Displayed inputs reproduce displayed results
  • L8: Literal check-command selection of the report tests

Prioritized next actions

PriorityOwning fileMissing evidence and action
1tests/reporting/test_vessel_seakeeping_module_report.pySupply external equation and coefficient references alongside the scalar oracle; make text I/O explicit and add actionable rebuild diagnostics. Retain a run with PYTHONUTF8 unset.

10scripts/reporting/report_evidence_audit.pyResolve unmapped historical and supporting pages before expanding report-specific conclusions. Exact draft wording gaps need context and maintainer review, not bulk replacement.

Known limits

  • No report generator, engineering module, solver or report-specific test was executed.
  • Conditions and unfamiliar syntax are not evaluated; UNKNOWN is not evidence of omission.
  • Citation metadata does not establish source rights or physical correctness. The filename inventory excludes other document formats and test golden fixtures.

  • Dashboard integration, report fixes, capability inventory and knowledge-pack acceptance are SKIPPED.

The JSON companion retains the frozen file set, source digests, command locators and manual observations separately from generated summaries.
